war_measures = {
	r56_GER_decision_promote_hans_von_luck = {
	
		icon = generic_tank
	
		cost = 50
        fire_only_once = yes
		
		days_remove = 20
		
		custom_cost_text = promote_general_decision_cost
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			has_army_experience > 25
			has_political_power > 50
		}
		
		allowed = {

		}
	
		available = {

					NOT = {
						has_focus_tree = turkish_focus
					}

					has_completed_focus = TUR_the_mediterranean_entente
					has_completed_focus = TUR_readdress_the_montreux_convention
				}
				
			}
		}
		
		remove_effect = {
			create_corps_commander = {
				name = "Hans von Luck"
				gfx = "GFX_portrait_GER_hans_von_luck"

				id = 75
				attack_skill = 2
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 3
				logistics_skill = 3
			}
		}

			army_experience = -25				
			add_political_power = -50
		}
	}

	GER_reforge_the_stahlhelm = {

				limit = { has_idea = GER_wilhelm_iv_2 }

GER_trade_influence_decisions = {
	GER_pressure_LIT = {	#as a food exporter, you cant really influence them, only extort their trade reliance
		allowed = {
			original_tag = GER
		}
		fire_only_once = yes #once per country
		available = {
			LIT = {
				ic_ratio = {
					tag = ROOT
					ratio < 0.2
				}
				is_in_faction = no
				has_war = no
				is_neighbor_of = GER
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = {
					has_completed_focus = GER_molotov_ribbentrop_pact #just to make clear what this below is about
				}
				NOT = {
					has_global_flag = sov_yes_pact
				}
				has_global_flag = GER_used_original_MR
			}
		}
		visible = {
			LIT = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = {
					owns_state = 188
				}
			}
		}
		cost = 25
		complete_effect = {
			LIT = {
				country_event = r56.ger.34
			}
		}
	}

	GER_influence_nation = {
		allowed = {
			original_tag = GER
		}
		fire_only_once = yes #once per country
		targets = { AUS HUN YUG ITA ROM }
		#Austria: Trade Reliance on Germany
		#Hungary: Trade reliance on Germany
		#Yugoslavia: Trade reliance on Germany
		#Romania: Germany historically paid extra for Romanian goods to artificially become a major trade partner
		#Italy: if the historical situation happens of Italy fighting a civil war in Germany's faction, puppeting Mussolini's regime would be cool

		#unlikely to make sense:
		#Poland: weak economy and limited trade
		#Latvia: Food exporter and self-sustainable
		#Lithuania: has own decision as its hard to influence food production
		available = {
			#if = {
			#	limit = {
			#		FROM = {
			#			original_tag = YUG
			#		}
			#	}
			#	NOT = {
			#		FROM = {
			#			has_idea = anti_german_military
			#		}
			#	}
			#}
			OR = {
				FROM = {	#this double FROM seperates puppeting of Allies and puppeting of others
					ic_ratio = {
						tag = ROOT
						ratio < 0.3
					}
					is_in_faction_with = ROOT
					NOT = {	#if someone is conquering your faction member you dont get to puppet them without joining that war (pretty sure thatd cancel it)
						any_enemy_country = {
							NOT = {
								has_war_with = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				FROM = {	#hostile takeover - put indicators here of very heavy reliance/exerted pressure even if not too friendly
					ic_ratio = {
						tag = ROOT
						ratio < 0.2
					}
					is_in_faction = no
					has_war = no
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = ROOT
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						original_tag = ITA
					}
					has_civil_war = yes
					ic_ratio = {
						tag = ROOT
						ratio < 0.3
					}
					is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		cost = 25
		complete_effect = {
			FROM = {
				country_event = r56.ger.34
			}
		}
	}

	GER_coup_nation = {	#probably use to unlock spy mission for LaR users and event chain for non-LaR (or hide)
		allowed = {
			original_tag = GER
		}
		fire_only_once = yes #once per country
		targets = { AUS HUN YUG ITA ROM }
		available = {
			FROM = {
				is_ai = yes
				has_country_flag = GER_target_for_hostile_trade_influence
				OR = {	#ideologies suitable for influential business overthrow
					AND = {
						NOT = {
							has_government = fascism
						}
						fascism > 0.3
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = {
							has_government = neutrality
						}
						neutrality > 0.3 
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "La Resistance"
					NOT = {	#cant get network
						FROM = {
							is_in_faction_with = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				network_strength = {
					target = FROM
					value > 70
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = ROOT
				}
				has_country_flag = GER_target_for_hostile_trade_influence
				NOT = {	#country can take decently small penalty to avoid government influence
					has_country_flag = GER_country_cut_all_ties
				}
			}
		}
		cost = 25
		complete_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { #coup progress, AI capitulates, any player could resist in civil war likely to be won
					FROM = {
						country_event = r56.ger.39
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						FROM = {
							ROOT = {
								country_event = r56.ger.41
							}
						}
					}
				}
				50 = { #botched coup
					FROM = {
						country_event = r56.ger.40
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						FROM = {
							ROOT = {
								country_event = r56.ger.42
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	GER_protect_interests_nation = {	#probably use to unlock spy mission for LaR users and event chain for non-LaR (or hide)
		allowed = {
			original_tag = GER
		}
		fire_only_once = yes #once per country
		targets = { LIT AUS HUN YUG ITA ROM }
		available = {
			FROM = {	#intentionally duplicate with visible for tooltip purposes
				has_country_flag = GER_target_for_hostile_trade_influence
			}
		}
		visible = {
			NOT = {
				has_wargoal_against = FROM
				is_in_faction_with = FROM
				has_war_with = FROM
			}
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
				has_country_flag = GER_target_for_hostile_trade_influence
			}
		}
		cost = 25
		complete_effect = {
			create_wargoal = {
				type = puppet_wargoal_focus
				target = FROM
				expire = 90
			}
		}
	}

					}
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = GER_coalition_cost_modifier }
			}
			clr_country_flag = GER_no_gov
			IF = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GER_nraf_coa}
				complete_national_focus = GER_nraf_in_coalition
			}
			IF = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GER_dnvp_coa}
				complete_national_focus = GER_dnvp_in_coalition
			}
			IF = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GER_dvu_coa}
				complete_national_focus = GER_dvu_in_coalition
			}
			IF = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GER_spd_coa}
				complete_national_focus = GER_spd_in_coalition
			}
			IF = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GER_kpd_coa}
				complete_national_focus = GER_kpd_in_coalition
			}
			IF = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GER_dstp_coa}
				complete_national_focus = GER_dstp_in_coalition
			}

				complete_national_focus = GER_zentrum_in_coalition
			}

			clr_country_flag = GER_dnvp_coa
			clr_country_flag = GER_dstp_coa
			clr_country_flag = GER_zentrum_coa
			clr_country_flag = GER_spd_coa
			clr_country_flag = GER_kpd_coa
			IF = {
				limit = { has_elections = no}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = ROOT
					elections_allowed = yes
				}
			}
			create_pie_chart = yes
		}

			base = 1000
		}

								}
							}
							CZE = {
								has_country_flag = HUN_AH_CZE_refused
							}
						}
					}

					}

							is_subject = no
						}
						NOT = { has_war_with = ENG }

						CZE = {

							NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER }
							NOT = { has_war_with = ENG }
							OR = {
								controls_state = 69
								controls_state = 1078
								controls_state = 74
							}
						}
						OR = {
							HUN = {
								NOT = {
									has_completed_focus = HUN_protect_czechoslovakia #DOD only
								}
							}
							CZE = {
								has_country_flag = HUN_AH_CZE_refused
							}
						}
					}
					ENG = {
						country_event = germany.40
					}
				}

				### EVENT CHAIN - ALT-HIST DEMAND OF SUDETENLAND TO CZE OR THEIR OVERLORD ###
				else_if = { # If not historical and CZE is independent or GER subject, send event to them
					limit = {
						CZE = {
							OR = {
								is_subject = no
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					CZE = {
						country_event = { id = germany.122 }
					}
				}
				else_if = { # If not historical and CZE is subject of someone else, send event to overlord
					limit = {
						CZE = { is_subject = yes }
					}
					CZE = {
						overlord = { country_event = { id = germany.122 } }
					}
				}
			}

			### EVENT CHAIN - ALT-HIST SOME ONE ELSE CONTROLS SUDETENLAND STATES ###
			if = { #If other countries control Sudetenland states -> send event to them or overlord
				limit = {
					any_other_country = {
						NOT = { tag = CZE }
						OR = {
							controls_state = 69
							controls_state = 1078
							controls_state = 74
						}
						NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					}
				}

					limit = {
						NOT = { tag = CZE }
						OR = {
							controls_state = 69
							controls_state = 1078
							controls_state = 74
						}
						NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					}

							OR = {
								is_subject = no
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							}
						}
						country_event = { id = germany.1041 days = 4 random_days = 2 }
					}
					else = { #If subject of someone else, send event to overlord
						if = {
							limit = {
								overlord = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
							}
							overlord = { country_event = { id = germany.1041 days = 4 random_days = 2 } }
						}
					}
				}
			}

			add_named_threat = { threat = 5 name = GER_demand_sudetenland_threat }
			set_country_flag = GER_sudetenland_demanded
		}

	GER_colonial_border_war = { 
		icon = GFX_decision_border_war
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		state_target = yes

		targets = { 773 777 546 768 769 895 894 541 893 743 647 1096 737 684 633 634 646 726 725}

		available = {
			FROM = {
				controller = {
					NOT = {
						is_ally_with = ROOT
					}
					NOT = { 
						has_non_aggression_pact_with = ROOT
					}
					ROOT = {
						can_declare_war_on = PREV
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = GER_colony_active}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				controller = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = ROOT
						has_non_aggression_pact_with = ROOT
						NOT = { ROOT ={ can_declare_war_on = PREV}}
						NOT = { 
							FROM = {
								any_neighbor_state = {
									is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						original_tag = ROOT
						has_war_with = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			FROM = {
				controller = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }}
				any_neighbor_state = {
					is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
				}
				controller = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = ROOT}
				}
				controller = { NOT = { original_tag = ROOT}}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_command_power = -25
				add_political_power = -25
			}
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = KMT_no_border_conflict
				days = 45
				value = 1
			}
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			command_power > 24
			has_political_power > 24
		}
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_CP_25_pp_25

		days_remove = 15

		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = GER_start_border_war_tt
			set_variable = { FROM.defender_state_vs_@ROOT = FROM }
			FROM = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = GER_defender_state_tt
				random_neighbor_state = {
					limit = {
						is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = GER_attacker_state_tt
					ROOT = { set_variable = { FROM.attacker_state_vs_@ROOT = PREV }}
				}
			}
			start_border_war = {
				change_state_after_war = yes
				attacker = {
					state = var:FROM.attacker_state_vs_@ROOT
					num_provinces = 4
				}
				
				defender = {
					state = var:FROM.defender_state_vs_@ROOT
					num_provinces = 4
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				OR = {
					has_government = fascism
					AND = {
						has_government = neutrality
						has_elections = no
					}
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = GER_colony_active
				days = 10
				value = 1
			}
		}
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = FROM
			}
		}
	}
